# Engine technology matrix (merged from the research reports)

Generated by `python3 tools/research/merge_matrix.py` from `docs/reassessment/research/matrix/R*.json`. Do not edit by hand: change the source rows (or the report that owns them) and re-run the tool. `--check` verifies that this file and `matrix/merged.json` are up to date.

Evidence labels are those of the source rows: VERIFIED / REPORTED / HYPOTHESIS / UNKNOWN / DISPROVED / NOT FOUND (BRIEF rule 3). The merge adds no claims of its own. Where two reports disagree, both values are kept and the field is marked **CONFLICT**; complementary values from several reports are listed per source as `[R5#3]` (report R5, row 3 of `matrix/R5.json`, counting from 0).

## Validation findings

Rules: all 23 keys present and no others; `key_code` a list of strings, every other value a string; `url` a URL or `NOT FOUND`; `sha` 40 lower-case hex or starting with `UNKNOWN` / `NOT FOUND`; `status` one of active, stale, abandoned, not-found; the factual fields (models_supported, hardware, offload_methods, scheduler, kernels, multi_gpu, long_context_262k, overlap_gpu_cpu_ssd, benchmarks, independent_confirmation, limitations) carry at least one evidence label (a value starting with `n/a` makes no claim). Errors make a row invalid. Warnings: non-canonical labels (`NOT-FOUND`, `CONFIRMED`, lower case), unlabelled judgements in portability_to_us, adaptation_cost, testable_without_target_hw, potential_impact (read as HYPOTHESIS), `last_activity` without a date, `license` naming no known licence, status inconsistent with url/sha, VERIFIED claims with an empty `key_code`. The source JSON files are not modified by this tool.

| offload_methods | VERIFIED: VRAM expert cache in a cuMemMap elastic arena + all routed experts in pinned RAM (lossless repack Q4L/Q4X/IQ4L/Q8_0), CPU computes misses or GPU reads them zero-copy; attention KV paged through pinned RAM above 32K (docs/maintainer/flash-next-engine.md §3.1, §3.9) |
| scheduler | VERIFIED: one CUDA graph per decode step (1-4 tokens); per layer GPU writes ids to a mapped ExpertLink and raises a flag, host thread spins, runs CpuExperts::run, raises a flag (engine.cpp:1721 host_experts); decode-time cache adaptation, 192 swaps/step max (engine.cpp:2335 AdaptParams) |
| kernels | VERIFIED: own CUDA GEMV/GEMM/QSA/expert kernels (src/flashnext/cuda/*.cu); CPU expert kernels AVX-512 only (src/flashnext/cpu_experts.cpp: 223 lines with _mm512), no AVX2 path |

| long_context_262k | REPORTED: 262K window, needle at 250K retrieved, 45.1 tok/s plain / 44.9 MTP at 250K depth (flash-next-engine.md §4.5, 1x4090 + 7950X) |
| overlap_gpu_cpu_ssd | VERIFIED: GPU cached experts \|\| CPU uncached experts in the same layer; background swaps on own stream; no SSD tier for experts (GGUF mmap for PLE) |
| benchmarks | REPORTED: 1x RTX 4090 + Ryzen 9 7950X DDR5 (55-62 GB/s), UD-IQ4_XS 94 GB: 50-60 tok/s short, ~44 at 256K, prefill 2,500-3,000 tok/s (README; flash-next-engine.md §2, §4.5) |
| independent_confirmation | NOT FOUND: single author's runs on one box |
| limitations | VERIFIED: AVX-512 host kernels (Zen 3 EPYC has AVX2 only); single GPU; no Q4_K/Q5_K experts so UD-Q4_K_XL does not load; UD-IQ4_XS has IQ3_S gate/up (&lt;4 bit, research/README.md); Windows-first tooling |
| key_code | `src/flashnext/engine.cpp:host_experts — per-layer CPU side of the GPU/CPU expert handshake`<br>`src/flashnext/engine.cpp:AdaptParams (Decode-time adaptation) — decayed-use expert swaps between steps`<br>`src/flashnext/vram.cpp — cuMemCreate/cuMemMap elastic expert arena`<br>`src/flashnext/cpu_experts.cpp:CpuExperts::run — AVX-512 VNNI expert GEMV over lossless repacks`<br>`src/flashnext/cuda/kv_stream.cu — attention KV paged from pinned RAM`<br>`docs/maintainer/flash-next-engine.md — design and measurements` |
